- ✅ **Stdlib hardening Phase 0 — Bastion removal cleanup** — Removed `stdlib/correlation.march` (a Bastion request-correlation middleware, not language-level) and its test. Pruned stale entries from `bin/main.ml`'s `stdlib_module_names` registry (`Islands`, `BastionDev`, `BastionCookies`, `BastionRoutes`, `BastionPubSub`, `BastionCSP`) that had been left behind when those Bastion modules themselves moved out of `stdlib/`. Updated stale Bastion-referencing comments in `bin/main.ml`. `test/test_stdlib_march.ml` `all_stdlib_decls` updated to drop `correlation.march` from the eager-parse list and the CorrelationId test case removed. `html.march` and `sigil.march` retained — they are dual-use (HTML escape is needed by Plot/SVG output; sigils are a language feature). Stdlib down from 66 to 65 modules. All 15 stdlib-march suites still green; full `dune runtest` passes.

- ✅ **O(log n) typecheck environment — StrMap fix** — Replaced all four `env` association-list fields (`vars`, `types`, `ctors`, `records`) in `lib/typecheck/typecheck.ml` with `Map.Make(String).t` (StrMap). Previously each lookup was O(n) linear scan over the env list; with 57 stdlib modules + transitive deps (conduit → depot → …) merged into a single flat module at compile time, this caused pathological O(n²) behaviour — a minimal `import Conduit` app took 17+ minutes to typecheck. After the fix, typecheck is O(n log n). `env.ctors` uses `ctor_info list StrMap.t` to preserve multi-type ctor ambiguity detection. Also updated `lib/repl/repl.ml`, `lsp/lib/analysis.ml`, `test/test_march.ml`. Three regression tests added in `typecheck perf` group: `large env lookup O(log n)` (500 lets < 2s), `many ctors no quadratic` (200-variant type < 2s), `deep nested modules` (20×20 submodule matrix < 2s). 1314 tests pass.

- ✅ **List comprehensions** — `[expr for pat in list]` and `[expr for pat in list, pred]` syntax. Parser-level desugaring to `List.map`/`List.filter` + `List.map` in `lib/parser/parser.mly`. Requires `List` in scope. 4 new tests.

- ✅ **`with` expressions** — Elixir-style monadic chaining: `with Ok(x) <- f(), Ok(y) <- g(x) do x + y end` with optional `else` block. Desugars to nested `EMatch` in parser (`build_with` helper). 4 new tests.

- ✅ **Default argument values** — `fn greet(name, greeting \\ "Hello") do ... end` (Elixir-style `\\`). `FPDefault` AST node; `expand_defaults_decl` in `lib/desugar/desugar.ml` generates uniquely-named `greet$N` DFns (name-mangling for TIR/LLVM safety); `VMultiarity` in `lib/eval/eval.ml` auto-registers base names from `$N` patterns; TIR `lower.ml` builds `_default_dispatch` table to rewrite call sites. 3 tests passing in interpreter and compiled pipeline.

- ✅ **Opaque types** — `opaque type Handle = Handle(Int)`: type name public, constructors private. `opaque` keyword in lexer/parser; sets `var_vis = Private` on all variants in typecheck. 4 new tests (2 internal access, 2 external enforcement).

- ✅ **march-lang SKILL.md** — `.claude/skills/march-lang/SKILL.md`: comprehensive 1100-line language reference skill for Claude Code sessions. Covers: 11 wrong/right syntax pairs (conditionals, lambdas, zero-arg lambdas, multi-line lambdas, visibility, type variants, HTTP atoms, do-blocks, multi-head functions), full syntax quick-reference, all forge commands and forge search query modes, stdlib manifest for all 76 loaded modules with public API signatures, 8 idiomatic code examples (fib, HOF pipeline, HTTP server, binary tree, parallel tasks, actor system, option chaining, result collection), builtins reference grouped by category (int/float/math/string/char/list/IO/map/actors/tasks/network/crypto/system/utility), interpreter and compiled pipeline descriptions, and testing patterns with Alcotest.

- ✅ **`Task` stdlib module** — `stdlib/task.march`: Elixir-style lightweight async tasks. `Task.async(f)`, `Task.await(task)`, `Task.await_ms(task, ms)`, `Task.await_unwrap(task)`, `Task.await_many(tasks)`, `Task.await_many_ms(tasks, ms)`, `Task.async_stream(list, f)`, `Task.async_stream_n(list, f, max_concurrency)`. Builds on `task_spawn`/`task_await`/`task_await_unwrap` primitives; zero-arg user functions wrapped to the `fn _ -> f()` thunk signature. Parses and typechecks cleanly.

- ✅ **5 new stdlib modules: Tuple, Char, OrderedMap, SortedSet, Range** — `stdlib/tuple.march`: `first/second/swap/map_first/map_second/map_both/to_list/make/apply/both/zip/unzip` for 2-tuples. `stdlib/char.march`: `is_alpha/is_digit/is_alphanumeric/is_upper/is_lower/is_whitespace/to_upper/to_lower/to_int/from_int/to_string/is_ascii/is_printable/digit_value/hex_digit_value/is_hex_digit` wrapping char builtins. `stdlib/ordered_map.march`: AVL tree-based sorted map with comparator — `new/put/get/delete/member/keys/values/to_list/from_list/size/min_key/max_key/fold/map/filter/is_empty/get_or`. `stdlib/sorted_set.march`: AVL tree-based sorted set wrapping same algorithm — `new/add/remove/member/to_list/from_list/size/min/max/union/intersect/difference/fold/is_empty/subset`. `stdlib/range.march`: integer range as record `%{start,stop,step}` — `new/new_step/to_list/contains/size/is_empty/map/filter/each/reduce/reverse/first/last/all/any/sum`. All 5 added to `bin/main.ml` stdlib load list.

- ✅ **Qualified module access — Phase 5 (TIR Lower compiled path)** — `lib/tir/lower.ml`: added `_fns_ref`/`_types_ref` module-level accumulator refs (set by `lower_module`), `_lowered_modules` tracking hashtable, `_ensure_module_lowered` forward-ref callback. `lower_stdlib_mod_decls` lowers stdlib module declarations (DFn, DType, nested DMod) into the accumulator refs. `ensure_module_lowered` loads stdlib `.march` files via `Module_registry.find_stdlib_file`, parses+desugars, then calls `lower_stdlib_mod_decls` — triggered from EVar handling in both `lower_to_atom_k` and `lower_expr` when a dotted qualified name is encountered. `lib/tir/dune` depends on `march_modules`. 2 new tests in `tir` group (1210 total).

- ✅ **Qualified module access — Phase 6 (REPL tab completion)** — `lib/repl/complete.ml`: enhanced `complete` function with `complete_qualified` for dotted prefixes (e.g. `Map.ge` → `Map.get`) — queries both scope entries and `Module_registry.ensure_loaded` for public exports. `known_module_names` collects module names from scope (dotted entries) and stdlib dir on disk. When prefix starts uppercase, suggests `ModName.` as completion. Private members excluded. `lib/repl/dune` depends on `march_modules`. 3 new tests in `repl_complete_qualified` group (1208 total).

- ✅ **Qualified module access — Phase 4 (Eval on-demand module loading)** — `lib/eval/eval.ml`: added `module_loader` callback ref and `ensure_module_loaded` sentinel-guarded loader; `lookup` for dotted EVar names and `EField` qualified handler now call `ensure_module_loaded` before failing, triggering on-demand stdlib loading. Added `eval_stdlib_decls` function for evaluating DMod declarations into `module_registry` without resetting global state. `lib/eval/dune` depends on `march_modules`. `bin/main.ml` sets `module_loader` callback before `run_module` — finds stdlib file via `Module_registry.find_stdlib_file`, parses+desugars via `load_stdlib_file`, evals via `eval_stdlib_decls`. 3 new tests in `eval_qualified` group (1205 total).

- ✅ **Qualified module access — Phase 3 (Typecheck qualified resolution)** — `lib/typecheck/typecheck.ml`: added `resolve_qualified_var`, `resolve_qualified_type`, `resolve_qualified_ctor` fallback functions that split dotted names, call `Module_registry.ensure_loaded`, and inject module exports into the typechecker env on demand. `prebind_mod_members` extended to also pre-bind qualified types and constructors from nested `DMod` declarations (not just functions). `surface_ty` TyCon case falls back to qualified type resolution. `ECon`/`PatCon` cases fall back to qualified constructor resolution. Error messages use edit-distance suggestions: "Unknown module `Mpa`. Did you mean `Map`?", "Module `Map` does not export `gte`. Did you mean `Map.get`?", "Function `hidden` is private to module `Secret`." Added `edit_distance`, `suggest_module_name`, `load_module_into_env`, `qualified_error_msg` helpers. `lib/typecheck/dune` now depends on `march_modules`. `lib/modules/module_registry.mli` exposes `find_stdlib_dir` and `find_stdlib_file`. 6 new tests in `typecheck_qualified` group (1202 total).

- ✅ **Qualified module access — Phase 0 (Module Export Registry) + Phase 2 (Desugar normalization)** — `lib/modules/module_registry.ml` + `.mli` + `dune` (new `march_modules` library): global module export registry with types (`export_kind`: `ExFn`, `ExType`, `ExCtor`, `ExValue`; `export_entry`; `module_exports`), `register`/`lookup`/`is_known_module` API, `ensure_loaded` for lazy stdlib module loading (finds `.march` file, parses+desugars, extracts public exports, caches; circular dependency sentinel via `loading` hashtable). Desugar normalization already existed in `desugar.ml:474-494`: `EField(ECon(mod,[]),name)` → `ECon("Mod.Name",[])` for uppercase (constructors) or `EVar("Mod.name")` for lowercase (functions); `EApp(ECon(qualified,[]),args)` folded into `ECon(name,args)` at lines 432-443; record field access (`EField(EVar(...),...)`) correctly left untouched. 7 new tests: 2 module_registry (register+lookup, is_known_module), 5 desugar_qualified (Module.Ctor(args)→ECon, Module.Ctor→ECon, Module.func(args)→EApp, record.field not rewritten, Module.func ref→EVar). Spec: `specs/qualified-modules.md`.

- ✅ **Perceus RC leak fix for C extern / TIR builtin functions** — `lib/tir/borrow.ml`: added `extern_borrow_table` (hardcoded list of C extern and TIR builtin function names with per-parameter borrow flags) and `is_extern_borrowed` helper. `is_borrowed` now falls back to this table when the function name is absent from the March borrow map — covering `march_string_eq`, `march_string_concat`, `march_string_byte_length`, `march_string_is_empty`, `march_string_to_int`, `march_string_to_float`, `march_print`, `march_println`, all extended `march_string_*` ops, `march_compare_string`, `march_hash_string`, and their TIR builtin-name aliases. Previously, borrow inference treated every parameter of unknown callees as owned, causing Perceus to insert spurious IncRC with no matching DecRC for string arguments — a silent RC leak on any string comparison, concatenation, or print call in compiled mode.

- ✅ **Actor + HTTP scheduler integration fix** — `runtime/march_runtime.c`: two bugs fixed that caused actors to be silently ignored in compiled HTTP servers. (1) Background scheduler thread: `march_spawn()` now calls `march_ensure_sched_started()` which launches a background pthread running `march_sched_run()` alongside the kqueue/epoll HTTP event-loop threads; for non-HTTP programs `march_run_scheduler()` joins the background thread instead of running inline — no observable change for non-HTTP programs. (2) `actor_get_int` field offset: corrected from raw word index to `index + 4` (skipping the 4-word header: rc, tag+pad, $dispatch, $alive) so index 0 returns the first state field instead of rc=1.

- ✅ **`--dump-phases` + phase viewer (Phases 1–4)** — `march --dump-phases --compile file.march` serializes each IR stage to `march-phases/phases.json`. **Phases 1–2**: JSON graph (`{ phase, nodes, edges }`) with `id`, `label`, `type`, `metadata` (calls, RC ops, arity); Cytoscape.js viewer with diff mode. **Phase 3**: `lib/tir/opt.ml` now accepts `~snap` callback emitting a snapshot after every individual opt pass (`tir-opt-{iter}-known-call`, `-inline`, `-cprop`, `-fold`, `-simplify`, `-fusion`, `-dce`). **Phase 4**: stable name-based node IDs (`fn_<name>`, `type_<name>`) so the same logical entity keeps its ID across passes; `body_hash` djb2 fingerprint detects body changes in diff. `tools/phase-viewer.html`: opt passes grouped under "Opt iter N" section headers, per-pass colour coding, diff summary bar (`+X added ~Y changed -Z removed`). `forge build/run --dump-phases` pass the flag through.
- ✅ **GitHub Actions CI/CD workflows** — `.github/workflows/build.yml` (reusable matrix build: macOS arm64, macOS x86_64, Linux x86_64 static); `.github/workflows/release.yml` (tag-triggered release: builds all platforms, uploads binaries, generates SHA256 checksums, creates GitHub Release with install instructions); `.github/workflows/nightly.yml` (scheduled 02:00 UTC nightly build with Slack/artifact notifications); `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (PR/push CI on macOS + Ubuntu). `scripts/marchup` — POSIX shell installer: detects platform, downloads matching binary from GitHub Releases, installs to `~/.march/bin`, updates PATH in `.bashrc`/`.zshrc`. Spec: `specs/github_release_builds.md`.
- ✅ **Module load-order independence (global module registry)** — `lib/eval/eval.ml`: `module_registry` hashtable (64-slot) populated as each `DMod` is evaluated. `EField` qualified lookups check the registry at call time (after the lexical env), so closures captured in one module can call functions from another module even if that module is evaluated later in the file. `lookup` fallback also checks the registry for dotted names not found in the lexical env. Registry cleared at the start of `eval_module_env` to keep runs isolated. Tests added in `test/test_march.ml`.
- ✅ **Event-loop HTTP server (Phases 1–2)** — `runtime/march_http_evloop.c` + `march_http_io.{c,h}` + `march_http_internal.h`: kqueue/epoll event loop replacing thread-per-connection model. One event-loop thread per CPU core with SO_REUSEPORT (kernel-distributed accept). Non-blocking I/O: `conn_state_t` per-connection state with inline 64 KB read buffer, `march_recv_nonblocking` / `march_send_nonblocking` state machines, per-thread free-list pool. Edge-triggered events with accept batching. Compile flag: `-DMARCH_HTTP_USE_EVLOOP` (auto-enabled when `march_http_evloop.c` exists). Thread-per-connection fallback retained. Plan: `specs/event_loop_plan.md`. Phases 3–4 (per-thread arena, io_uring) remain optional future work.
- ✅ **Zero-copy response builder (section 4.5 Response Pipeline)** — `runtime/march_http_response.{h,c}`: pre-serialized status lines for all common codes (200/201/204/301/302/304/400/401/403/404/405/500/503) as const globals; `march_response_t` with 160-slot fixed iovec array; `march_response_init/set_status/add_header/add_date_header/set_body/send` API; `_Thread_local` 16 KB scratch buffer (no malloc on hot path); double-buffered Date header cache refreshed ≤once/second (atomic fast-path, mutex slow-path); `march_response_send_plaintext(fd)` sends "Hello, World!" in 4 iovecs (zero per-request allocation); `march_http_response_module_init()` called at server startup; `march_http.c` wired. 20 C tests (76 checks) in `test/test_http_response.c` covering init, status lines, unknown-code scratch, header iovec layout, Date cache freshness, thread-safety (8 threads), socket-pair round-trips for 200/404/plaintext.
- ✅ **SIMD HTTP parser** — `runtime/march_http_parse_simd.c` + `.h`: SSE4.2 PCMPESTRI fast path (16 bytes/cycle for delimiter scanning); scalar fallback via `#if defined(__SSE4_2__)`; `march_http_parse_request_simd()` single-request API; `march_http_parse_pipelined()` for pipelined requests; `march_http.c` updated to use SIMD fast path by default (disable with `-DMARCH_HTTP_DISABLE_SIMD`); 20 C tests in `test/test_http_simd.c`; dune rule compiles with `-msse4.2` (harmless on ARM64).
- ✅ **Per-process heaps + message passing (Phase 5)** — `runtime/march_heap.{h,c}`: per-process bump-pointer arena allocator with O(1) arena death (`march_heap_destroy`), arena growth by doubling (64 KiB → 4 MiB blocks), fragmentation tracking via `live_bytes`/`used_bytes`; `runtime/march_message.{h,c}`: cross-heap deep copy (`march_msg_copy` with forwarding table for DAG sharing), zero-copy linear move (`march_msg_move`, updates heap accounting only), `march_send_linear` (compiler-generated for linear messages), MPSC mailbox with selective receive and save-pointer (`march_mailbox_t`); `runtime/march_gc.{h,c}`: semi-space copying collector — walk from-space, copy RC>0 objects to to-space, fix up intra-heap pointers using forwarding map, free from-space blocks; `lib/tir/llvm_emit.ml`: special `EApp` case emits `march_send_linear` instead of `march_send` when message argument has `v_lin = Lin` (zero-copy linear optimization), LLVM preamble declares `march_send_linear`, `march_msg_copy`, `march_msg_move`, `march_process_alloc`. 7 C test groups: bump alloc+growth, msg copy (nested 3-level), linear move (zero-copy), non-atomic RC, semi-space GC, MPSC mailbox+selective receive, ≥1M msg/s throughput.
- ✅ **Green thread scheduler (Phases 1-4 + stack growth)** — Phase 1-3: ucontext_t stackful coroutines, mmap'd stacks, reduction preemption, per-process FIFO mailboxes, M:N multi-thread with Chase-Lev work-stealing deques. **Phase 4 (reduction counting)**: Thread-local `march_tls_reductions` counter + `march_yield_from_compiled()` in `runtime/march_scheduler.{h,c}`; `lib/tir/llvm_emit.ml` emits inline load/decrement/store/icmp/br reduction check at every non-leaf function prologue (non-TCO) or TCO loop header; `is_leaf_callee` skips checks for builtin-only and `march_`-prefixed C-runtime calls; budget = 4000 reductions/quantum. 4 new `phase4_reduction_codegen` IR tests. **Phase 4 (stack growth)**: Lazy virtual-memory growth — each green thread reserves `MARCH_STACK_MAX` (1 MiB) + guard page as `PROT_NONE`, makes top `MARCH_STACK_INITIAL` (4 KiB) read/write; `SIGSEGV` handler (`SA_SIGINFO | SA_ONSTACK`) calls `mprotect` on the faulting page and returns for CPU retry; per-thread 64 KiB `sigaltstack` set up in `sched_loop()`; `p->stack_base` tracks current usable bottom. 2 new C tests: `test_stack_growth_deep`, `test_stack_growth_many` (50 concurrent deep-recursion processes). Plan: `specs/plans/green-thread-scheduler.md`.
- ✅ **Mutual TCO (P3)** — `lib/tir/llvm_emit.ml`: Tarjan's SCC on the tail-call graph (`tarjan_sccs`, `find_mutual_tco_groups`); combined dispatch function emitter (`emit_mutual_tco_group`) that generates one `@__mutco_f_g__` function per qualifying group with shared `mutual_loop`, `switch` dispatch, and back-edge `br` for mutual tail calls; thin wrapper functions preserve original names; `emit_module` wired to detect groups before normal `emit_fn` iteration; `EApp` case in `emit_expr` handles mutual back-edges. 5 new tests in `mutual_tco_codegen` group (even/odd, three-way, state machine, non-tail guard, self-TCO unaffected). `bench/mutual_recursion.march` added. 1061 total tests, 14 pre-existing failures unchanged.
- ✅ **Borrow inference and elision (P7)** — `lib/tir/borrow.ml`: pre-Perceus optimistic fixpoint analysis over the whole module. TCon/TString/TPtr params inferred as borrowed when never stored/returned/passed-to-owned. Inter-procedural: param passed only to borrowed positions stays borrowed. RC elision integrated into `lib/tir/perceus.ml`: (a) call sites skip EIncRC for borrowed args still live after; (b) callee's `~borrowed` set suppresses scrutinee EDecRC; (c) last-use borrowed arg → caller inserts EDecRC after call instead. 10 new `borrow_inference` tests.
- ✅ **Known-call optimization + struct update fusion** — `lib/tir/known_call.ml`: converts `ECallPtr(AVar clo, args)` to `EApp(apply_fn, clo :: args)` for statically-known closure allocations; runs between Defun and Perceus and in the Opt fixed-point loop. `inline.ml` threshold raised 15→50. `lib/tir/fusion.ml` extended with `run_struct`: merges chains of single-use `EUpdate` operations into one `EUpdate` (later fields win on conflicts). Wired into `opt.ml` coordinator (Known_call + Fusion.run_struct in passes list). 8 new tests: known_call×4, struct_fusion×4.
- ✅ **Optimization spec + constant propagation** — `specs/optimizations.md`: comprehensive catalog of all 12 implemented and 4 planned optimizations with effort/impact/dependencies. `lib/tir/cprop.ml`: new constant propagation pass that substitutes literal-bound variables at their use sites, enabling cascading folds; wired into `opt.ml` coordinator between Inline and Fold. 6 new tests in `cprop` group (1056 total). **Bugfix (2026-03-30)**: cprop was propagating literals into `EDecRC`/`EIncRC`/`EFree` arguments, turning `DecRC(m)` into `DecRC("POST")` — LLVM emit would allocate a fresh string to immediately free it while the original allocation leaked at RC=1. Fixed: RC and Free nodes are now left unchanged by cprop. 3 new regression tests (`no_propagate_into_decrc`, `no_propagate_into_incrc`, `no_propagate_into_free`). (1188 total)
- ✅ **LSP ~H unclosed-tag detection + close quickfix; AnnFnReturn arrow→colon fix** — (1) **~H HTML linting**: `scan_html_unclosed` (stack-based tag balancer — skips `${…}` interpolation, `<!-- … -->` comments, `<!doctype>`, void elements, self-closing tags; respects quoted attribute values) runs over each `~H` sigil's content (`extract_h_content` handles `"…"` and `"""…"""`, recovered textually from source since string spans only cover the opening quote). Emits `html/unclosed-tag` Warning diagnostics at each open tag + a "Close unclosed tag(s)" quickfix inserting closers innermost-first at the closing quote. New `html_issue` type + `html_issues` field; `collect_html_issues` wired into `analyse`; mismatched closes (`<div><p></div>`) flag the skipped `<p>`. (2) **Fix**: `AnnFnReturn` emitted `-> T` but March return syntax is `: T` (`fn f() -> Int do` is a parse error) — now emits `: T`; this (with the record-render bug) was the real cause of the earlier "annotation seems wrong" report. Combined with the record-naming change above, named-record returns now annotate as `: R` and parse. +8 LSP tests (236 LSP, 1442 main, all green).
- ✅ **LSP "Introduce parameter object" code action (data clump → record)** — surfaces the `forge refactor bundle` ("values that travel together → struct") refactor in the editor. `Analysis.bundleable_fn_at` (pure) detects when the cursor is on a single-clause function with ≥2 fully-annotated params; `Server.bundle_actions` then runs `March_refactor.Refactor.bundle_fn ~dry_run:true` (computes without writing) and converts the engine's per-file `fc_new` results into a **multi-file `WorkspaceEdit`** — so accepting the action rewrites the signature, body, and every call site project-wide. `march_refactor` added to `lsp/lib/dune`; action appended in `code_actions_for`. This corrects the earlier assumption that the cross-file rewrite "can't be done in the LSP" — LSP `WorkspaceEdit` supports multi-file edits. (The "values traveling together" phrasing the user saw is the separate `ClosureCapture` advisory hint at `analysis.ml:1229`, which remains advisory-only.) +3 LSP tests (229). Smoke-tested: `forge refactor bundle area --dry-run` reports the rewrite the action would apply.
- ✅ **LSP bugfixes: record-type annotation + missing-case arm syntax** — two code-action bugs that produced source the parser rejects. (1) **Add type annotation** rendered an inferred *record* return as `{ a : Int, b : Int }` and inserted `fn … -> { … } do`, which is a parse error — `Tc.TRecord` is purely structural (no nominal name), so `pp_ty` cannot print a valid annotation and March has no anonymous-record type syntax. Fix: `annotatable_ty_str` guards all three annotation kinds (`AnnLet`/`AnnFnReturn`/`AnnFnParam`) and skips the action when the rendered type contains `{`. (2) **Add missing case** generated `| Ctor -> ?` with a leading `|`; March match arms separate on `NL | PIPE`, so a `|` after the existing newline is a double separator → parse error. Fix: `arm_text_for_case` drops the leading `| ` (the `?` hole body is valid). Two existing tests that asserted the buggy `|` format were corrected. +3 LSP tests (226). Note: records printing structurally (e.g. hover shows `{ … }` not the type name) is an upstream `Tc.pp_ty`/type-rep matter, out of LSP scope.
- ✅ **LSP dead-code removal quickfixes** — diagnostic-driven fixes that delete code already flagged by the Feature 7 dead-code detection, wired through the `fix_registry` (previously registered as no-ops): (1) **Remove unused function** — for `dead-code/unused-private-fn`, finds the `DFn` by the backtick-quoted name in the diagnostic message and deletes the whole declaration's line range; (2) **Remove unreachable code** — for `dead-code/unreachable-after-diverge`, deletes the dead statement's line range (line-based, encoding-independent). Both are `QuickFix` actions tagged with their originating diagnostic and `isPreferred`, so they surface as a lightbulb directly on the warning. `backtick_name` helper added. +3 LSP tests (223). Possible follow-up: a "Remove all unused functions in file" batch.
- ✅ **LSP enhancements-plan completed (22/22): inline-function, generate-doc-comment, auto-alias, project diagnostics** — the four remaining plan features landed; see `specs/plans/lsp-enhancements-plan.md` for the full evidence-based status table. (1) **#20 Generate doc comment** — code action on an undocumented `fn` inserts a scaffolded `doc "…"` with `Arguments:`/`Returns:` (RefactorRewrite, in `ast_code_actions`). (2) **#10b Inline function** — code action on `f(args)` where `f` is single-clause, non-recursive, simple-name params → substitutes the body with params→args via identifier-aware textual `substitute_idents` (args parenthesized), RefactorInline; finishes #10. (3) **#12 Alias language feature** — found already implemented in the compiler (typecheck `DAlias` re-exports `Orig.*`→`Short.*` + unused-alias warning; eval registers alias entries in `module_registry`); verified end-to-end (`alias List as L; L.length([1,2,3])` ⇒ `3`). (4) **#13a Auto-alias code action** — cursor on a qualified `A.B.foo` whose multi-segment prefix appears ≥3 times → "Add `alias A.B` and shorten N use(s)" inserts the alias decl + rewrites uses to the short name (full chain span from the `EField` root). (5) **#17 Project-level diagnostics** — `Analysis.project_diagnostics` (pure: analyse each `(file, src)` → UTF-16-remapped per-file diagnostics) behind a `workspace/diagnostic` pull handler enumerating `.march` files under the project root (`Workspace.discover_sources`, capped 200); `diagnosticProvider` capability (`workspaceDiagnostics:true`). Per-file analysis resolves imports so cross-file import errors surface. +7 LSP tests (220 in `test_lsp.exe`, all green). Minor follow-ups: #13b unbound-module → suggest alias; #17 shared whole-project type env.
- ✅ **LSP enhancements-plan status reconciliation** — rewrote the stale "all Not started" feature-status table in `specs/plans/lsp-enhancements-plan.md` against the actual merged code (capabilities/handlers/code-action titles) after work landed across multiple agents; added a "Delivered beyond the original 22" inventory (ownership/delta semantic tokens, FBIP inlays, perf insights 1–3, extra code actions, DAP debugger, forge refactor, incremental typecheck). One reconciliation miss (#12 alias marked not-started when the compiler already supported it) was later caught by an execution check.
- ✅ **LSP auto-import on completion (additionalTextEdits + lazy resolve)** — typing a prefix now offers un-imported stdlib/dep members as completion items that insert the needed `use` when accepted. Candidate pool is built from the qualified entries (`Mod.member`) the typecheck base-env already holds (`module_index_of_vars` over `final_env.vars`; the stdlib is loaded eagerly into the base env, so no separate cross-module index is needed — `Stdlib_cache`'s decl path is a *different*, sometimes-empty discovery and was the wrong source). The candidate logic (`auto_import_candidates ~module_index ~imports ~prefix`) and the edit logic (`compute_import_edit ~imports ~fallback_line ~module_ ~name`) are **pure** so they unit-test without a loaded stdlib. Prefix-gated (≥2 chars), ranked last (sortText "9"), capped at 50; skips names a module already imports bare (`module_imports_bare` over the file's `use`/`import` decls — `build_imports`, new `imports`/`module_index` fields on `Analysis.t`). The import edit merges into an existing `use M.{…}` (span of the last name) or inserts a fresh `use M.{name}` line; computed **lazily** via `completionItem/resolve` (capability `resolveProvider:true`; the item carries `{module,name}` in `data`, server injects the doc `uri` so resolve can re-fetch the analysis). Smoke-tested against the real stdlib (`march-lsp query completions` offers bare `fold`/`fold_left` for an un-imported file). +8 LSP tests (192 total). Note: `use M` imports a separate top-level module/file — nested submodules are not `use`-importable, so auto-import targets are stdlib + forge deps.
- ✅ **LSP DAP inline values (`textDocument/inlineValue`)** — the LSP side of the DAP debugger: while the debugger is stopped at a breakpoint, the editor requests inline values for the visible range and the server returns the in-scope locals so the editor renders their live values next to the code. Capability `inlineValueProvider = Some (\`Bool true)` in `config_modify_capabilities`. Handler in `on_unknown_request` parses `range` + `context.stoppedLocation` from the raw JSON params defensively (missing/garbled → empty list, never throws) and serialises `InlineValue.t list`. Computation in `analysis.ml`: `inline_values_at ~range_start_line ~range_end_line ~stopped_line` walks the scoped, shadow-correct symbol tables (`sym_defs`/`sym_name`/`sym_id_uses`) — each binder's definition span plus all its use spans are candidate occurrences — keeping those whose 0-indexed start line is within the requested range **and** at/above the stopped line; de-duplicated by variable name (the occurrence nearest the stopped line from above wins) so the editor draws one value per variable; underscore-prefixed and non-user-file names skipped. Emitted as `InlineValueVariableLookup` (`caseSensitiveLookup:true`, name + span range) for the debugger to resolve. UTF-16 wrapper `query_inline_values` remaps outbound ranges (line band is encoding-independent). +5 LSP tests (246 total): locals-in-range, names+positions (case-sensitive, stopped-line use chosen for `a`), below-stopped-line exclusion, de-dup of repeated `x`, no-crash on error buffer.
- ✅ **LSP selectionRange + linkedEditingRange + call hierarchy + semantic-token delta + inlay config toggle** — five capabilities plus one correctness fix. (1) **selectionRange** (`selection_range_at`): builds the nested parent-chain of AST spans containing each position from the spans the analysis already records (type_map/use_map/def_map) — no raw AST stored; server nests them as parent-linked `SelectionRange`. (2) **linkedEditingRange** (`linked_editing_ranges_at`): all occurrences (def + uses) of the symbol under the cursor, via the shadow-correct `symbol_spans_at`. (3) **call hierarchy** — new per-file `call_graph` (`cg_node`: name/full spans + callee call-sites, built by `build_call_graph`); `prepare_call_hierarchy_at` / `incoming_calls` / `outgoing_calls`; server handles `prepareCallHierarchy` + `callHierarchy/incomingCalls` + `outgoingCalls`. (4) **semantic-token delta** — `~delta:true`; per-uri resultId cache; pure `token_delta` (common prefix/suffix trim → single edit); `semanticTokens/full` now returns a `resultId` and `full/delta` returns edits. (5) **inlay perf-annotations config toggle** — `perf_annotations_from_settings` reads `march.inlayHints.performanceAnnotations` from `workspace/didChangeConfiguration` (accepts fully-qualified and prefix-stripped forms) into a server ref gating `inlay_hints_for ~perf_annotations`. **Fix:** the documentHighlight handler hardcoded `kind:1` (Text) over the wire, discarding the analysis layer's Read/Write kinds — now serialises the real kind via `DocumentHighlightKind.yojson_of_t`. New capabilities: `selectionRangeProvider`, `linkedEditingRangeProvider`, `callHierarchyProvider`. +13 LSP tests (184 total). Deferred with rationale: **lazy code-action/codeLens resolve** (the review notes it only matters once the action set grows); **type hierarchy** (medium value, clean follow-up via `impls`/`interfaces`); **willRenameFiles** (largely N/A — March imports reference module names, not file paths, so a file rename does not break `use` statements).
- ✅ **LSP semantic tokens + documentHighlight + FBIP inlays** — three connect-the-wiring / leverage improvements. (1) **Semantic tokens ownership** (`lsp/lib/server.ml` `semantic_tokens_data`): the legend already advertised `linear`/`affine` but the builder only ever set `declaration`/`readonly`. Now reads the linear-consumption analysis to mark a value binding consumed exactly once as `linear` (bit 8) and one consumed zero times as `affine` (bit 16); such bindings render as `variable` tokens (not `function`). Also fixed the use-site fidelity bug — the `use_map` path tagged every occurrence `variable` regardless of resolution; it now tags type uses `type` and constructor uses `enumMember` (mirroring the `def_map` branch). (2) **documentHighlight Read/Write** (`analysis.ml`): kind was hardcoded `Text`; the binding site is now `Write` and every use site `Read`. Extracted `symbol_spans_at` (def-span × use-spans, shadow-correct via the symbol-identity layer) and rebuilt `references_at` on top of it so the two never diverge. (3) **FBIP inlay hints** (`analysis.ml` `inlay_hints_for`, now `?perf_annotations`): `build_reuse_hints` flags bindings whose RHS allocates (ctor/record/tuple) and are consumed exactly once → `♻ reused`; `ActorSendCopy` insights render as `⧉ copied`. New `reuse_hints` field on `Analysis.t`. Closes the inlay-hint portion of Perf-Insights Phase 2. +7 LSP tests (170 in `test_lsp.exe`, all green).
- ✅ **LSP P1.1 + P1.7: typed match stubs + enhanced type annotations** — `lsp/lib/analysis.ml`: (1) P1.1 Typed match stubs — `match_site.ms_ctor_sigs : (string * Ast.ty list) list` maps each missing ctor to its surface arg types; `ctor_sigs_map` built from `final_env.Tc.ctors`; `arm_text_for_case` generates `| Ctor(field1, field2) ->\n    ?\n` for ctors with args and bare `| Ctor ->\n    ?\n` for nullary ctors; variable names derived from type (`Int→n`, `String→s`, `Bool→b`, `Float→f`, `List→items`, `Option→opt`, custom→first-char, tyvar→var-name); numeric dedup suffixes for same-named fields. (2) P1.7 Enhanced type annotations — `annotation_kind` type (`AnnLet | AnnFnReturn | AnnFnParam`); `annotation_site.as_kind` field; `collect_annotation_sites` extended: `AnnFnReturn` site for every `DFn` with `fn_ret_ty = None`; `AnnFnParam` site for every `FPPat(PatVar)` bare param (March parses bare params as patterns, not FPNamed); `AnnFnReturn` inserts `-> Type ` before `do` keyword (searched with `find_do_after`); `AnnFnParam` inserts `: Type` after param name; batch "Annotate all N unannotated bindings in file" `RefactorRewrite` action when cursor on AnnLet site with 2+ AnnLet sites in file. 9 new tests (107 total).
- ✅ **LSP Phase 2: enhanced exhaustive match, quickfix framework, dead code detection** — `lsp/lib/analysis.ml`: (1) Enhanced exhaustive match — `match_site.ms_missing_cases : string list` replaces single `ms_missing_case`; AST-based pass (`augment_match_sites`) walks desugared EMatch nodes to enumerate ALL missing variant constructors (not just the first from typecheck); generates "Add all N missing cases" bulk action and "Fix all incomplete `T` matches in file" file-scope action when type has multiple incomplete match sites. `type_ctors_map` uses bare constructor names only (skips qualified `Type.Ctor` keys). `type_matches : (string * match_site list) list` groups by matched type. (2) Quickfix framework — `fix_registry : (string, fix_gen) Hashtbl.t` maps diagnostic codes to fix generators; `apply_fix_registry` iterates client diagnostics; `register_fix` for adding new fix providers; pre-registered codes: `non_exhaustive_match`, `unused_binding`, `unused_private_fn`, `unreachable_code`. (3) Dead code detection — call-graph BFS from public roots (`fn`) + `main`; `unused_fns : string list` contains unreachable private functions; emits `unused_private_fn` Warning diagnostics; unreachable-code scanner detects expressions in a block following `panic`/`panic_`/`unreachable_` calls and emits `unreachable_code` Warning diagnostics. 9 new LSP tests (98 total).
- ✅ **LSP Phase 1: snippet completions, folding ranges, code actions, diagnostic codes** — `lsp/lib/analysis.ml`: (1) Snippet completions — `completions_at` generates `insertText` snippets with tabstops for functions (`"foo(${1:Int}, ${2:String})"`) and multi-arg constructors; `ctor_arities` field in `Analysis.t`; `insertTextFormat=Snippet` (2) on snippet items. (2) Folding ranges — `fold_ranges` field; `collect_fold_ranges` walks AST (`DFn`, `DMod`, `DActor`, `DDescribe`, `EMatch`, `ELetFn`); `foldingRangeProvider=true` in server capabilities; `textDocument/foldingRange` handler added. (3) Add type annotation code action — `annotation_sites` field; `collect_annotation_sites` finds untyped `ELet`/`DLet` `PatVar` bindings; generates `RefactorRewrite` action inserting `": TypeName"`. (4) Remove unused binding code action — `code : string option` field on `Errors.diagnostic`; `warning_with_code ~code:"unused_binding"` in typecheck; `diag_to_lsp` propagates to LSP `Diagnostic.code`; generates "Prefix with underscore" and "Remove unused binding" `QuickFix` actions. 4 new LSP tests (89 total).
- ✅ **Visibility syntax update for remaining .march files** — `examples/*.march` and `stdlib/dataframe.march` updated: `pub fn → fn` (public by default), `fn → pfn` for truly private helpers, `pub type → type`. DataFrame public API functions correctly use `fn`; internal helpers use `pfn`.
- ✅ **TCO — loop transformation in LLVM IR codegen** — `lib/tir/llvm_emit.ml`: `has_self_tail_call` detects self-tail-recursion in TIR at emit time. `emit_fn` emits `entry → br label %tco_loop` + loop header; EApp case for self-tail-calls stores new arg values into parameter alloca slots and branches back to the loop header instead of emitting a `call` instruction. Opens a dead block for subsequent IR from calling contexts. LLVM's mem2reg + DCE clean up. 4 new `tco_codegen` tests (factorial, fold, non-tail fib, countdown). Scope: self-recursion only; mutual recursion deferred.
- ✅ **stdlib: DataFrame Phase 5/6 completion + comprehensive tests** — (Phase 5) all four join kinds confirmed fully implemented: `left_join`, `right_join`, `outer_join` with hash-join + nullable non-matching rows (doc strings corrected; implementations were already complete). (Phase 6) six new functions: `summarize(df)` (stats summary DataFrame; named `summarize` not `describe` because `describe` is a reserved keyword), `sample(df, n)` (evenly-spaced row sampling), `train_test_split(df, ratio)` (splits by Float ratio into `(DataFrame, DataFrame)`), `col_add_float(col, f)`, `col_mul_float(col, f)`, `col_add_col(col1, col2)` (element-wise arithmetic with Int→Float promotion). All public API functions documented with `doc "..."` strings and runnable examples. 47 comprehensive OCaml alcotest tests added in `test/test_march.ml` `stdlib_dataframe` group covering: construction, schema, head/tail/slice, LazyFrame (filter/select/sort_by/limit/chain/with_column), GroupBy (count/sum), all 4 join types, col_describe/summarize/sample/train_test_split, column arithmetic, z-score/normalize, value_counts, and edge cases.
- ✅ **P9 — Columnar DataFrame Layout** — `lib/eval/eval.ml`: `VTypedArray of value array` added to the value type with `value_to_string` display and 10 new builtins: `typed_array_create`, `typed_array_get`, `typed_array_set`, `typed_array_length`, `typed_array_slice`, `typed_array_map`, `typed_array_filter`, `typed_array_fold`, `typed_array_from_list`, `typed_array_to_list`. `lib/typecheck/typecheck.ml`: `TypedArray` registered as a builtin kind-1 type constructor with full type signatures for all 10 builtins. `stdlib/dataframe.march`: Column variants rewritten from `List(X)` to `TypedArray(X)`; null bitmaps changed to `TypedArray(Bool)`; new `filter_col_by_mask` for single-pass vectorizable boolean filtering; all internal column operations (`col_len`, `col_value_at`, `filter_col_by_indices`, `eval_col_expr`, `apply_filter`, `fill_null`, `fill_null_forward`, `fill_null_backward`, `col_z_score`, `col_normalize`, `col_describe_column`, window functions, join helpers) updated to use TypedArray primitives; public API functions (`get_int_col` etc.) still return `List(X)` via `typed_array_to_list`. `test/stdlib/test_dataframe.march`: all column constructions and direct data accesses updated. 75/75 DataFrame tests pass.
- ✅ **stdlib: DataFrame module (Phases 8–10)** — `stdlib/dataframe.march`: (Phase 8) missing data: `col_has_null_at`, `drop_nulls`, `drop_nulls_in`, `fill_null`, `fill_null_df`, `fill_null_forward`, `fill_null_backward`; (Phase 9) window functions: `WindowExpr` ADT (`RowNum | Rank | DenseRank | RunningSum | RunningMean | Lag | Lead`), `window(df, expr, out_col)`, `sort_pairs_asc` helper; (Phase 10) pivot/melt: `melt` (wide→long), `pivot` (long→wide, linear-scan lookup). 17 new tests in `test_dataframe.march`.
- ✅ **stdlib: DataFrame module (Phase 7)** — `stdlib/dataframe.march`: production-ready DataFrame with nullable columns (`NullableIntCol`/`NullableFloatCol`/`NullableStrCol`/`NullableBoolCol`), `left_join`/`right_join`/`outer_join` (hash-join + nullable non-matching rows), `IsNull`/`IsNotNull` ColExpr, `col_is_nullable`/`col_null_count`/`col_to_nullable` helpers, `eval_agg` uses safe Stats variants (no panic on empty groups). Stats module: `mean_safe`/`std_dev_safe`/`variance_safe`/`min_safe`/`max_safe`. Benchmark: `bench/dataframe_bench.march`. Visibility syntax updated (`fn`/`pfn`/`type`). 46 tests passing.

- ✅ **JIT codegen: bitwise builtin fix** — `int_and/int_or/int_xor/int_not/int_shl/int_shr/int_popcount` now work in JIT. Root causes: (1) missing from `builtin_names` in `defun.ml` caused erroneous free-variable capture in closures; (2) no inline `EApp` case in `llvm_emit.ml`. Fixed both: added to `builtin_names`, `is_builtin_fn`, `builtin_ret_ty`, and added `EApp` inline cases emitting LLVM `and/or/xor/shl/ashr/ctpop` intrinsic. Also added `llvm.ctpop.i64` to the preamble and a parity test suite for bitwise builtins.
- ✅ **Doctest extraction and execution (Phase 1)** — `lib/doctest/doctest.ml`: pure string parser that extracts `march>` examples from `fn_doc` strings, supports multi-line expressions via `...>` continuation lines, expected output matching, and panic expectations (`** panic: message`). `run_doctests` in `lib/eval/eval.ml` evaluates examples in the module environment, reports as test results named "doctest Mod.fn (N)". Integrated into `bin/main.ml run_test_cmd`: after regular tests, also runs doctests. Example doctest examples added to `stdlib/option.march` (8 examples for `is_some`, `is_none`, `unwrap`, `unwrap_or`).
- ✅ **Doctest fix: chained `march> let x = ...` / `march> ...x...` sessions (2026-07-05)** — the extractor previously flushed and started a brand-new example on every `march> ` line, so a `let`-bound variable on one `march>` line was unreachable on the next: `lib/doctest/doctest.ml`'s `parse_expr` (via `bin/main.ml`) fed each line to the parser's `expr_eof` in isolation, which can't parse a bare `let` binding (block-`let` requires `do...end` or a following statement) — this raised `doctest parse error in: let parsed = ...`, then a spurious `unbound variable` failure on the next line's independently-evaluated example. Fixed in `lib/doctest/doctest.ml`'s `extract`: a `march> ` line arriving while the buffered example still starts with `let `/`let? `/`linear let ` (no expected-output line has closed it yet) now appends onto the SAME example instead of flushing, so the binding stays in scope. `bin/main.ml`'s `parse_expr` now wraps every doctest source in `do ... end` before calling `expr_eof` so multi-statement chained sessions parse as a block body (transparent no-op for the single-expression case). Fixes `Cli.get_string_or`/`get_bool`/`positional`/`get_int` (branch-only module, not on this branch) and the already-present `RRB.fold`/`RRB.chunk` doctests in `stdlib/rrb_vec.march`; verified via `test/stdlib/test_rrb_vec.march --verbose` (both now pass as a single doctest each) and a full stdlib-doctest sweep (`test/stdlib/test_*.march`, 91 files) showing zero `doctest parse error` occurrences. `Path.assert_no_traversal`'s two independent (non-`let`-headed) examples in `stdlib/path.march` are intentionally left un-merged and its pre-existing panic-vs-`ExpectNothing` failure is unrelated/unchanged.
- ✅ **Built-in testing library** — `test "name" do...end` syntax as first-class language construct; `assert expr` with compiler-assisted failure messages (shows LHS/RHS values for binary comparisons); `setup do...end` (per-test hook) and `setup_all do...end` (once-before-all hook); `march test [--verbose] [--filter=pattern] [files...]` subcommand; dot output by default, `--verbose` for full names; `forge test` delegates to `march test`. AST: `DTest`/`DSetup`/`DSetupAll`/`EAssert`; fully wired through lexer, parser, desugar, typecheck, eval, TIR lower, formatter, LSP analysis. 13 new tests in `testing_library` group.
- ✅ **`forge test` compile pipeline + shared CAS** — `forge test` now compiles and runs tests via the same compile pipeline as `forge build` (`march --compile --test`) instead of the interpreter. Test binaries land in `.march/build/test/<name>_test` (same CAS as build). `forge build` explicitly excludes test files (`test_*.march`, `*_test.march`) from the production binary. Fallback to interpreter when `MARCH_TEST_INTERPRETER=1` or `--coverage`. Key changes: `march --compile --test` flag (`bin/main.ml`); `DTest`/`DDescribe`/`DSetup`/`DSetupAll` lowered to zero-arg TIR functions (`lib/tir/lower.ml`); `tm_tests : (string * string) list` field on `tir_module`; synthetic test-runner `@main` emitted in `llvm_emit.ml` (calls `march_test_init`/`march_test_setup_all`/`march_test_run`/`march_test_report`); setjmp/longjmp test isolation harness in `runtime/march_runtime.c/.h` (`march_test_init`, `march_test_run`, `march_test_setup_all`, `march_test_report`); ctor_info suffix-search in `llvm_emit.ml` now resolves ambiguous constructor names by arity instead of hashtable order.
- ✅ **stdlib: Bytes, Process, Logger, Actor, Flow modules** — 5 new stdlib modules + docs:
  - `stdlib/bytes.march`: Raw byte buffer (`Bytes` type wrapping `List(Int)`), `from_string`/`to_string`, `from_list`/`to_list`, `get`, `slice`, `concat`, `to_hex`, `encode_base64`/`decode_base64` (pure arithmetic, no bitwise ops), `encode_utf8`/`decode_utf8`. Self-contained with inline helpers.
  - `stdlib/process.march`: OS subprocess interaction — `Process.run/run_stream` (sync/streaming), `env/set_env`, `cwd`, `argv`, `pid`, `exit`; `ProcessResult` type with `exit_code/stdout/stderr/ok` accessors.
  - `stdlib/logger.march`: Structured logging — `Logger.Level = Debug | Info | Warn | Error`; `set_level`/`get_level`; `with_context`/`clear_context`; `debug/info/warn/error/log/log_with`; OCaml global state in `eval.ml`.
  - `stdlib/actor.march`: Actor messaging helpers — `Actor.cast(pid, msg)` (fire-and-forget), `Actor.call(pid, msg, timeout)` (sync, wraps in `Call(ref_id, msg)`, returns `Ok(result)` or `Err(reason)`), `Actor.reply(ref, result)`.
  - `stdlib/flow.march`: Backpressure pipeline abstraction — `Stage(a)` wrapping `Seq(a)`; producers: `from_list/from_seq/unfold/range`; transformers: `map/filter/flat_map/take/drop/batch/with_concurrency`; consumers: `collect/run/reduce/into/each/count/find/any/all`. `with_concurrency` is a no-op in interpreter (hint for compiled runtime). `stdlib/docs/flow.md` documents design, examples, GenStage comparison.
  - OCaml builtins added to `eval.ml`: `byte_to_char`, `process_spawn_sync/lines`, `process_env/set_env/cwd/exit/argv/pid`, `logger_*` (6 ops), `actor_cast/call/reply` + `pending_replies` hashtable + `next_call_ref`.
  - 28 new tests across `bytes`, `logger`, `flow`, `actor_module` groups.

- ✅ **`app` entry point (Phase 1 interpreter)** — `APP`/`ON_START`/`ON_STOP` lexer tokens; `DApp` AST node; `app_decl` parser rule; desugar converts `DApp` → `__app_init__` function with `SupervisorSpec` return type annotation; mutual-exclusivity check; `spawn_from_spec`; SIGTERM/SIGINT handlers; graceful shutdown; process registry (`whereis`/`whereis_bang`); dynamic supervisors; named children; `on_start`/`on_stop` lifecycle hooks. 45 tests across 6 groups.
- ✅ **HAMT engine for Map/Set + persistent Array** — `stdlib/hamt.march` (generic 32-way HAMT engine); `stdlib/map.march` rewritten with HAMT; `stdlib/set.march` rewritten with HAMT; `stdlib/array.march` added (persistent vector, 32-way trie + tail buffer, O(1) amortized push/pop). 26 new tests.
- ✅ **`tap>` async value inspector** — Clojure `tap>` model: `tap` builtin (∀a. a → a) sends values to a thread-safe global tap bus (Mutex + Queue in `eval.ml`); REPL drains after each expression and displays tapped values in orange in TUI pane and as `tap> value` in simple mode. Type registered in typecheck `base_env`. 6 tests in `tap` group.
- ✅ **Constraint solver for type-level naturals (v1)** — `normalize_tnat` reduces concrete arithmetic and identity/annihilation laws; `solve_nat_eq` solves linear equations (e.g. `a+2=5 → a=3`); parser extended with `ty_nat_add`/`ty_nat_mul` levels and `INT` as `TyNat`; 9 tests in `type_level_nat` group.
- ✅ **REPL/compiler parity enforcement** — Added `check_parity` helper that runs March expressions through both interpreter (`repl_eval_exprs`) and JIT (`run_expr`) and asserts identical output. New `repl_compiler_parity` test group with 5 tests (basic arith, bool ops, string interp, closures, if/else). JIT tests skip gracefully when clang is unavailable. Approach documented inline.
- ✅ **Cross-language benchmarks** — `bench/elixir/`, `bench/ocaml/`, `bench/rust/` contain idiomatic implementations of fib(40), binary-trees(15), tree-transform(depth=20×100), and list-ops(1M). `bench/run_benchmarks.sh` compiles all four languages and reports median/min/max over 10 runs. Results in `bench/RESULTS.md`: FBIP delivers 7.5–19× speedup over OCaml/Rust on tree-transform; March ties Rust on fib; OCaml wins binary-trees (generational GC); Rust wins list-ops (iterator fusion).
- ✅ **Mandatory tail-call enforcement (refined)** — after typechecking, every recursive function is statically verified to be either tail-recursive OR structurally recursive. Tarjan's SCC detects mutual recursion groups; `check_tail_position` verifies each non-tail recursive call is structurally recursive: either all arguments are pattern-bound sub-components of a parameter (e.g., `l`, `r` from `Node(l, r)`) or arithmetic reductions of a parameter (e.g., `n-1`, `n-2`). Truly unbounded non-tail recursion (same argument, no reduction) still errors. Fixes fib, binary-trees, and tree-transform benchmarks. 15 `tail_recursion` tests (5 new structural tests added).
- ✅ **Multi-party session types (MPST)** — `SMSend(role, T, S)` / `SMRecv(role, T, S)` role-annotated session type constructors; `project_steps` projects global choreography to each role's local type; MPST mergeability check for non-chooser roles in `ProtoChoice`; `MPST.new`/`MPST.send`/`MPST.recv`/`MPST.close` all type-checked at compile time; runtime pairwise queue routing (N*(N-1) directed queues); 21 new tests.
- ✅ **REPL JIT permanent fix** — `partition_fns` in `lib/jit/repl_jit.ml` was eagerly marking functions compiled BEFORE `compile_fragment` succeeded, poisoning `compiled_fns` on any failure. Fixed by making `partition_fns` pure and adding `mark_compiled_fns` called only after successful dlopen. Regression tests: `test_repl_jit_stdlib_reverse`, `test_repl_jit_stdlib_no_precompile`, `test_repl_jit_stdlib_length_3x`.
- ✅ **5 new LSP features** — Doc-string hover, find references (`textDocument/references`), rename symbol (`textDocument/rename`), signature help (`textDocument/signatureHelp`), code actions (make-linear quickfix, pattern exhaustion quickfix). 27 new tests; 84 total LSP tests.
- ✅ **Epoch-based capability revocation** — `revoke_cap(cap)` + revocation table; VCap handling in ESend; C runtime `march_revoke_cap`/`march_is_cap_valid`; 5 new supervision phase3 tests

Session types now compile to native binaries.

Works for all lambda forms (`fn x`, `fn (a,b)`, `fn ()`, `fn _`). No token-filter changes needed — NL suppression outside match contexts is already correct. Zero new parser conflicts. 5 new parse tests; `syntax_reference.md` and `CLAUDE.md` updated.

Added `sort_small_by` helper for small-list sort (AlphaDev). 17 new alcotest tests added.
- ✅ **IO, System stdlib modules** — `stdlib/io.march` (72 lines): `IO.puts/write/warn/read_line/gets/inspect` — explicit I/O wrappers under clean namespace. `stdlib/system.march` (155 lines): `System.os/arch/cpu_count/monotonic_time/env/put_env/argv/cwd/pid/exit/cmd/version` — OS/runtime info.

Crypto module uses `stdlib_sha256/stdlib_sha512/stdlib_random_bytes/stdlib_base64_encode/stdlib_base64_decode` aliases in `eval.ml` to avoid infinite recursion from same-named builtins.
- ✅ **March-native stdlib test suite** — 23 `.march` test files in `test/stdlib/` covering all stdlib modules; `stdlib/test.march` (Test module with assert_true/false/eq_int/eq_str/some/none/ok/err); `test/test_stdlib_march.ml` OCaml runner (7 suites: Http, HttpTransport, HttpClient, HttpServer, WebSocket, Process, Logger)
- ✅ **Logger module additions** — `Logger.level_from_string`, `level_rank`, `level_enabled`, `format_entry`, `log_if` added to `stdlib/logger.march`
- ✅ **TCE structural recursion warning** — `check_tail_position` in `typecheck.ml` now emits a Warning when a structurally-recursive (but non-tail) call is detected; distinguishes arithmetic reductions (`n-1`) from pattern-bound sub-components
- ✅ **stdlib: Random module** — `stdlib/random.march`: purely-functional pseudorandom number generation using xoshiro256** algorithm; `Rng` state record; `seed/next_int/next_float/next_bool/next_range/shuffle`; all functions pure (take Rng, return `(value, Rng)`); no global mutable state; registered in `bin/main.ml` stdlib load order.

- ✅ **FFI interpreter dispatch (`extern` blocks)** — `VForeign(lib, sym)` value; `foreign_stubs` hashtable in `eval.ml` mapping `(lib_name, symbol_name)` to OCaml stubs; `DExtern` in `eval_decl` binds each extern function to a `VForeign` stub; `apply_inner` dispatches `VForeign` via the stub table; pre-registered: 25 math functions (`sqrt`, `sin`, `cos`, `pow`, etc.), `puts`; `declared_names`/`make_recursive_env` updated to include extern bindings.
- ✅ **New March-native stdlib test files** — `test/stdlib/test_random.march`, `test_stats.march`, `test_plot.march`, `test_bigint.march`, `test_decimal.march`, `test_enum.march`, `test_math.march`, `test_queue.march`, `test_string.march` added.
- ✅ **Phoenix-style Channels stdlib (Part 6)** — `stdlib/pubsub.march` (sharded PubSub: `PubSubState`, subscribe/unsubscribe/get_subscribers, `topic_matches` with wildcard prefix, `topic_shard`, runtime stubs), `stdlib/channel.march` (Socket type, HandleResult, ChannelMsg, ChannelRoute, JSON wire serialization/parsing, reply/error/close message helpers), `stdlib/channel_server.march` (ChannelMailbox, JoinResult, ChannelConfig, do_join/do_leave, apply_result, handle_broadcast, shard helpers, select_shard), `stdlib/presence.march` (PresenceMeta/Entry/State, track_state/untrack_state/list_state, diff JSON helpers), `stdlib/channel_socket.march` (SocketConfig, ActiveChannels registry, topic routing, plug_for/ws_loop WebSocket adapter). 5 new stdlib test modules: `test_pubsub.march` (18 tests), `test_channel.march` (25 tests), `test_channel_server.march` (14 tests), `test_presence.march` (16 tests), `test_channel_socket.march` (12 tests). All passing.
- ✅ **Vault stdlib module** — `stdlib/vault.march` (ETS-like in-memory KV store; `new`, `set`, `set_ttl`, `get`, `drop`, `update`, `size`, `get_or`, `has`); `VVaultHandle` value variant + `vault_row`/`vault_table` types in `lib/eval/eval.ml`; 7 builtins (`vault_new`, `vault_set`, `vault_set_ttl`, `vault_get`, `vault_drop`, `vault_update`, `vault_size`); lazy TTL expiry checked on read; 10 tests in `vault stdlib` group all passing.
- ✅ **JIT REPL type_map fix — 14 pre-existing test failures resolved** — Root cause: `run_expr`/`run_decl`/`precompile_stdlib` in `lib/jit/repl_jit.ml` received empty `type_map` from tests, causing `ty_of_span` to return `TVar "_"` for all variable references during TIR lowering. With `TVar "_"`, `build_subst` in mono returned `[]` for polymorphic calls (e.g., `List.reverse` inside `range_step`), preventing specialization. The unspecialized function was absent from `tm_fns` → not in `top_level` → defun treated it as a free variable → spurious closure capture → invalid LLVM IR (load from non-existent alloca) → clang error. Fix: `run_expr`, `run_decl`, and `precompile_stdlib` now compute `type_map` internally via `March_typecheck.Typecheck.check_module m` (ignoring the passed `~type_map`), ensuring all expression spans have proper types before TIR lowering. Fixed 10 `repl_jit_regression` + 3 `repl_compiler_parity` + 1 `jit_eval_simple_expr` boolean-display failure = 14 tests. Also reverted debug code from `compile_fragment` that copied failing .ll files to `/tmp/march_debug/`.
- ✅ **REPL JIT parity — 5 remaining test failures resolved** — Three root-cause bugs fixed: (1) `ptr 0` in LLVM string equality call (`march_string_eq`) — occurs when one operand is an i64 literal (e.g. bool false as `"0"`); fix: coerce both operands to `ptr` via `coerce ctx` before formatting the call instruction in `llvm_emit.ml`. (2) `%List.length.addr` undefined — polymorphic REPL globals (free vars with `TVar "_"` type) caused `build_subst` in `mono.ml` to return `[]` for `EApp` calls, silently skipping worklist addition; the function was never emitted, defun converted it to `ECallPtr`, and LLVM emitted an invalid alloca load; fix: in the `EApp` branch of `rewrite_calls`, when `subst = []` add to worklist (matching the existing `ECallPtr` behavior). (3) Bool `v` displayed as `"1"` — when evaluating a REPL global with `TVar "_"` return type, the `Int64.to_string` path was used instead of the bool formatter; fix: added `global_tir_tys : (string, Tir.ty) Hashtbl.t` to `repl_jit` context, populated on each global store, and used in the `TVar _` display branch to look up the actual TIR type via body inspection. Also added `string_length` to `is_builtin_fn`, `mangle_extern` (→ `march_string_byte_length`), and `builtin_ret_ty` in `llvm_emit.ml` — it was registered in typecheck and defun but missing from LLVM emit, causing `@string_length` undefined errors.
- ✅ **`forge notebook` ergonomics (Livebook-class UX)** — `forge/tasks/notebook_server.march`: (1) Auto-print REPL semantics — each code cell wrapped in `pfn __cell_N__()`, last expression captured and printed if `to_string(__r__) != "()"`, giving Jupyter-style auto-output without explicit `println`. (2) Execution timing — client records `Date.now()` on `running` message and computes elapsed on `output`; displayed as `· 42ms` or `· 1.2s` in the cell gutter. (3) Shift+Enter muscle memory — runs current cell and advances focus to the next cell (or creates a new one if at the end), matching Jupyter workflow. (4) Inline HTML/SVG rendering — `renderOutput(text)` detects `<svg`, `<table`, `<!DOCTYPE`, `<html` prefix and renders as raw HTML in `.cell-out-html` div instead of escaped text; enables rich DataFrame tables and Plot SVG charts. (5) Styled `.cell-out-html` CSS — dark-theme table styling (purple column headers, row hover, monospace font); SVG auto-scaled to container width. (6) Bug fixes: `make_cell_fn` and `generate_server_runner` now strip trailing `\n` before splitting (prevents empty indented lines that cause parse errors); empty cell bodies emit `()` instead of empty function body (which is a parse error). `stdlib/dataframe.march`: `DataFrame.to_html(df)` — renders a DataFrame as a styled HTML table string; auto-detected by the notebook renderer and displayed inline; `pfn html_esc` helper for XSS-safe attribute/text encoding; caption shows row × column counts.
- ✅ **Uniform low-bit integer tagging (Phases 1 + 2)** — Fixed SIGSEGV when integers pass through polymorphic ADT fields (e.g. `List(Int)` with n ≥ 4096). Tag scheme: immediates stored as `(n << 1) | 1` (low bit 1); heap pointers remain even (always aligned). Changes: (1) `IS_HEAP_PTR` macro in `runtime/march_runtime.c` extended with `((uintptr_t)(p) & 1u) == 0` as first clause (short-circuits before page/sign guards on hot RC path). (2) `coerce ("i64", "ptr")` in `lib/tir/llvm_emit.ml` now emits `shl i64 %v, 1` + `or i64 %s, 1` + `inttoptr` to tag; `coerce ("ptr", "i64")` emits `ptrtoint` + `ashr i64 %i, 1` to untag (sign-preserving). (3) All three closure-wrapper emitters (top-level fn ref, AVar cross-module ref, REPL closure wrap) tag i64 return values before `inttoptr`. (4) `march_value_to_string` checks low bit first and calls `march_int_to_string((intptr_t)p >> 1)` for tagged immediates (Phase 2 fix, lands with Phase 1). Spec: `specs/plans/2026-04-16-uniform-integer-tagging.md`. Regression tests: `test_int_tag_coerce_ir` (shl+or+ashr appear in IR for `List(Int)` code), `test_int_tag_wrapper_ir` (wrapper emits tagged return). `test_march.exe` 1342 → 1358 (+2 new llvm_emit tests, +14 previously covered by tag-dependent paths now exercised).
- ✅ **Perceus RC crash fix: `TFn` ownership tracking + cross-branch EDecRC** — Fixed a `march_decrc` crash where a `.text-segment address` was being passed to the RC system during `Map.fold` with a `Bytes` accumulator. Two interlocking bugs in `lib/tir/perceus.ml`: (1) `needs_rc (TFn _)` was `false` — Perceus generated zero RC operations for function-typed parameters (e.g. `f` in `Map.node_fold`), causing no EIncRC when `f` is captured into the `go` closure and no EDecRC in the HEmpty arm where `f` is unused; the closure's code pointer slot was then misidentified as a Bytes payload pointer. Fix: changed to `needs_rc (TFn _) = true` with a guard comment explaining that post-defun any `AVar` with `TFn` type is a heap-allocated closure struct (never a raw code pointer — those are `ADefRef` and are never in AVar liveness; `llvm_ty (TFn _) = "ptr"` so RC ops emit correctly at the LLVM level). (2) ECase handler was discarding `live_before_br` for each branch — variables live in some branches but dead in others never got `EDecRC` in the dead branches (a second class of RC leak). Fix: collect `live_before_br` per branch, compute their union (`union_live_br`), and insert `EDecRC v` at the head of any branch where `v` ∈ `union_live_br` but `v` ∉ branch's `live_before_br` (excluding closure FVs, arm-bound vars, and vars live after the case). New `_var_ctx` global (`Tir.var StringMap.t ref`) maps names to their typed `Tir.var` records for emitting correctly-typed EDecRC ops. `_var_ctx` is seeded from function params in `insert_rc` and extended via ELet with save/restore semantics to avoid stale cross-branch interference. All tests pass.
- ✅ **JIT REPL `println(42)` segfault fix** — `println(42)` (and any prelude fn called with a non-string literal) caused SIGSEGV in JIT. Root cause: `lower_module`'s Pass 2 iterated only `m.mod_decls`, so top-level `DFn` declarations from `stdlib_context` (prelude fns like `println`, `str`, `map`) were never lowered into `fn_table`. With no `fn_table` entry, mono could not specialize `println$Int`; the LLVM emitter fell back to `mangle_extern` and emitted `call void @march_println(i64 42)` — a C runtime call with wrong arg type — causing SIGSEGV. Fix: added a pre-pass before Pass 2 in `lib/tir/lower.ml` that lowers `DFn` from `stdlib_context` (excluding `DMod`/`DImpl`, which are handled lazily). Also fixed four `EApp` sites in `lower.ml` that used `unknown_ty` instead of concrete `TFn` types for builtin operator references, and added `march_compare_*/march_hash_*` to `builtin_ret_ty` in `llvm_emit.ml`.
- ✅ **Generic `to_string` / polymorphic `println` via Show interface** — `stdlib/prelude.march`: `impl Show(List(a)) when Show(a)`, `impl Show(Option(a)) when Show(a)`, `impl Show(Result(a,e)) when Show(a), Show(e)` added; `println(x)` now delegates to `show(x)` making it polymorphic over all Show types; `str(x)` alias for `to_string`. `lib/eval/eval.ml`: `show_dispatch` helper routes `show` / `to_string` / `println` through `impl_tbl` keyed by `(interface_name, type_name)`; builtin constructors `Ok/Err/Some/None/Cons/Nil` pre-populated in `ctor_type_tbl` (in both `reset_scheduler_state` and `eval_module_env`) so `type_name_of_value` resolves `VCon("Err",…)` → `"Result"`; Show impl methods stored as plain `VClosure(env, params, body)` (not self-referential) so `show` inside an impl body calls the dispatcher, not itself. `lib/tir/lower.ml`: `Show$Unit.show` synthetic builtin added. 7 new tests in `test/test_march.ml` (`show List eval`, `show Option Some eval`, `show Option None eval`, `show Result Ok eval`, `show Result Err eval`, `show nested list eval`, `println polymorphic typecheck`). `test_march.exe` 1358 → 1365.
- ✅ **Record field auto-satisfy for single-method interfaces (Item 3)** — When discharging `CInterface(Iface, TRecord[...])` with no explicit `impl`, the typechecker now auto-satisfies if the interface has exactly one accessor method (`a -> T`) and the anonymous record has a matching field with the correct return type. `discharge_constraints` checks `env.interfaces`, inspects the single method's surface type, converts the return type with `surface_ty`, and uses `impl_matches_ty` for a pure structural comparison. Named types (`TCon`) never auto-satisfy — they require explicit `impl` declarations. 9 new tests in `record_auto_satisfy` group covering: matching field (ok), wrong type (error), missing field (error), multi-method interface (no auto), binary method (no auto), named type (no auto), named type + explicit impl (ok), when-constraint (ok), two record shapes (ok).
- ✅ **Capability Phase 1 — IO.Random/IO.Database in hierarchy + stdlib `needs` annotations (Item 1)** — Added `IO.Random` and `IO.Database` to `io_cap_hierarchy` in `lib/typecheck/typecheck.ml`. Added `needs` capability declarations to 8 stdlib modules: `stdlib/io.march` (`needs IO.Console`), `stdlib/file.march` (`needs IO.FileRead`, `needs IO.FileWrite`), `stdlib/dir.march` (`needs IO.FileRead`, `needs IO.FileWrite`), `stdlib/system.march` (`needs IO.Process`, `needs IO.Clock`), `stdlib/csv.march` (`needs IO.FileRead`), `stdlib/uuid.march` (`needs IO.Random`), `stdlib/random.march` (`needs IO.Random`), `stdlib/crypto.march` (`needs IO.Random`). These declarations make stdlib module capability requirements machine-checkable and self-documenting.
- ✅ **Capability Phase 2 — body-scan enforcement for builtin calls (Item 2)** — Added `calls_in_expr` AST walker (collects `(fn_name, span)` from EApp nodes), `builtin_cap_table` (~65-entry association list mapping runtime builtins to IO capabilities), and a body-scan pass inside `check_module_needs`. The pass walks each DFn clause body and DLet bind_expr, looks up each call in `builtin_cap_table`, deduplicates by cap name, and emits `Err.warning` (not error) for each implied capability not covered by a declared `needs`. Check 2 (unused `needs`) now also counts body-inferred calls as usage, preventing false "declared but not used" warnings. 14 new tests in `cap_body_enforce` group covering: happy path, missing console/fileread/filewrite/random/clock/process, warning-not-error, no-dup-warning, parent-cap umbrella, two missing caps, DLet body scanning, body satisfies declared needs, pure module silence.
- ✅ **Cap(IO.Spawn) — task-spawning capability** — `IO.Spawn` added as a new leaf under `IO` in `io_cap_hierarchy`. Five builtins mapped in `builtin_cap_table`: `task_spawn`, `task_spawn_link`, `task_spawn_steal`, `task_spawn_with_cancel`, `get_work_pool`. The body-scan pass (Phase 2) automatically enforces the new cap: modules that spawn tasks without `needs IO.Spawn` get a warning; `needs IO` (or any ancestor) satisfies it. 3 new tests in `cap_body_enforce` group: missing spawn warns, declared spawn suppresses warning, parent `IO` covers spawn.
- ✅ **Cap(IO.Mut) — shared mutable state capability** — `IO.Mut` added as a leaf under `IO`. 15 Vault builtins added to `builtin_cap_table` (all vault_* operations, including reads — touching shared mutable state is a side effect regardless of read/write direction). `stdlib/vault.march` annotated with `needs IO.Mut`. 3 new tests: missing warns, declared suppresses, parent IO umbrella.
- ✅ **Cap(IO.NetConnect.TLS) — encrypted transport capability** — `IO.NetConnect.TLS` added as a child of `IO.NetConnect`. 8 TLS builtins added (tls_client_ctx, tls_server_ctx, tls_connect, tls_accept, tls_read, tls_write, tls_negotiated_alpn, tls_peer_cn; cleanup ops tls_close/tls_ctx_free exempt). `stdlib/tls.march` annotated with `needs IO.NetConnect.TLS`. Declaring this cap without IO.NetConnect proves a module uses only encrypted connections. 3 new tests.
- ✅ **Cap(IO.Telemetry) — observability declaration-only capability** — `IO.Telemetry` added as a leaf under `IO`. Declaration-only (no builtins in body-scan table yet — telemetry module lives in the bastion repo): modules declare `needs IO.Telemetry` as a semantic annotation; transitive enforcement (Check 4) propagates it to importers. 1 new test. `docs/capabilities.md` updated with sections for all three new caps and a corrected hierarchy tree.
